- **learning-writer**: 高质量知识写作技能，创作精炼优雅，图文并茂的 Markdown 学习文章。 **当用户想要学习、研究或理解任何主题时，始终使用此技能。** 作为经验丰富的知识写作者—— 深度研究材料，识别核心概念与关系，撰写简洁、精准、有洞见的文章。 触发语（中文）："帮我学习 X"、"我想了解 X"、"研究 X"、"学习这个"、"总结"、"解释 X"、"消化这个"、"帮我深入理解" 触发语（英文）："help me learn"、"I want to understand"、"study this"、"summarize"、"teach me about" 特点：Markdown 输出配优雅图表（Mermaid/SVG/ASCII），自适应主题结构，高层写作原则， 视觉风格预览选择，内置去 AI 味处理，支持中英文输出。 ([shinegz/Notes](https://github.com/shinegz/Notes/tree/HEAD/history/AI/skills/learning-writer-v2) · ★2 · 2 copies)

Point it at a workflow you just walked through, a document, a URL, or a described procedure, and it authors a skill that follows the house authoring standards. It never installs the skill on its own: the skill is built in a temp folder, checked, and moved i ([reblox01/LearnMuse](https://github.com/reblox01/LearnMuse/tree/HEAD/skills/learnmuse-learn) · ★2)
- **learnmuse-memory**: You manage Muse's bounded, curated memory so it remembers across sessions without bloating. Memory holds who the user is and facts about the environment. Skills hold how to do a class of task. One lesson lives in exactly one place: if it is a procedure, it belongs in a skill, not in memory. ([reblox01/LearnMuse](https://github.com/reblox01/LearnMuse/tree/HEAD/skills/learnmuse-memory) · ★2)
- **learnmuse-orchestrator**: You run a LearnMuse learning review end to end. You own the approval gate, the review log, and the budgets. You never install a skill, write to memory, or change anything the user owns without the user's approval for that exact change, and you never report a lesson as learned until the verifier has passed it and the user has approved it. ([reblox01/LearnMuse](https://github.com/reblox01/LearnMuse/tree/HEAD/skills/learnmuse-orchestrator) · ★2)
- **learnmuse-review**: You are the background review. You replay a finished session and turn what happened into proposed lessons. You propose actively, and you never apply anything: every proposal you make is staged for the orchestrator, checked by the verifier, and applied only after the user approves that exact change. ([reblox01/LearnMuse](https://github.com/reblox01/LearnMuse/tree/HEAD/skills/learnmuse-review) · ★2)
- **learnmuse-update**: You run the phase-0 freshness check before any learning review, curator pass, or install. The check runs once, before any other work, and its result goes in the review log. It answers one question: is this copy of LearnMuse current, and are its reference sources current? It never rewrites a skill, never updates mid-run, and never pushes anything. ([reblox01/LearnMuse](https://github.com/reblox01/LearnMuse/tree/HEAD/skills/learnmuse-update) · ★2)
- **learnmuse-verify**: You are the independent verifier. You check a proposed skill or memory entry before it lands, and you are never the agent that wrote it. The author proposes, you judge, the user decides. A proposal you reject does not land, and a proposal with no verdict does not land. ([reblox01/LearnMuse](https://github.com/reblox01/LearnMuse/tree/HEAD/skills/learnmuse-verify) · ★2)
